- **sci-council**: Three-persona mathematical research fan-out. Asks Gauss (algebraic / number-theoretic), Erdős (probabilistic / extremal), and Tao (harmonic / arithmetic-combinatorics) the same problem independently in parallel, then synthesises their 3×3 proposals into one ranked table with cross-persona consensus flags and composite scores. Use when a hard math problem needs breadth — stuck competitions, Einstein Arena openings, a new research question where a single frame is likely to miss something. - **sci-figure**: Extracts figures and sub-figures from academic PDF papers. - **sci-index**: 수집한 논문 폴더(papers/{paper_id}/)에서 서지정보 색인 index.csv를 만들고 검수한다. 편수와 관계없이 sci-retr 수집 뒤 항상 실행한다(LLM 없음). "색인 만들어", "논문 목록 정리해", "이 폴더에 무슨 논문 있어", "관련 논문 찾아줘"에 사용하며 리버·김리버·김리버씨·댕댕아·댕댕이·김댕댕·김댕댕씨 호칭이 앞에 붙어도 적용한다. 한국어 한 줄 요약은 사용자가 원할 때만 sci-tldr가 만든다. Router matches the user's problem description against recipe keywords and returns the best fit. - **sci-paper-revision**: Use when rewriting, polishing, de-AI-ing, compressing, or restructuring SCI manuscript sections while preserving verified numbers, citations, methods, and evidence boundaries ([keithhegit/sci-using-kit](https://github.com/keithhegit/sci-using-kit/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-paper-revision) · ★1)
- **sci-paper-writing**: 地理学、遥感、土壤、生态方向 SCI 英文研究论文的写作与修改。覆盖三类研究论文：时空变化（趋势、变化检测、驱动归因）、定量反演与模拟（土壤盐分、SOC、土壤水分、植被参数等）、观测对照（处理与对照、野外实验）。对标新锐分区地理学相关 1 区期刊（RSE、ISPRS J、TGRS、JAG、IJGIS、Catena、Geoderma、Ecological Indicators 等）。用于选题与创新点判断、选刊、标题、摘要、Highlights、引言、研究区、数据与方法、精度验证、结果、讨论、结论、图件、去 AI 味润色、cover letter 与投稿自查。用户提到写/改/润色/审查地理或遥感论文的任何部分、问"创新点够不够""投哪本刊""引言怎么写""讨论怎么写""SHAP 怎么写""怎么去 AI 味"时使用。综述与 Meta 分析用 geo-review-writing；回复审稿意见用 geo-reviewer-response；建立个人写作风格档案用 geo-writing-style。 ([Xiangyu-Ge/sci-writing-geors](https://github.com/Xiangyu-Ge/sci-writing-geors/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-paper-writing) · ★62)
- **sci-papers-downloder**: Search academic papers (OpenAlex no-key by default, or Scopus with a built-in key) and download full-text PDFs concurrently — OA direct link → Unpaywall → Sci-Hub fallback (on by default). - **sci-write**: High-quality scientific writing for research papers and grant/fellowship applications (MSCA-PF, DFG Eigene Stelle, Humboldt Fellowship, GFZ Discovery), tuned for seismology / natural hazards / AI-for-geoscience. Enforces the user's writing standard — precise flow of thought, deep technical engagement, no AI slop, no em-dash unless essential — plus a de-slop revision pass built from Wikipedia's "Signs of AI writing". Use whenever the user asks to write, draft, edit, review, tighten, or de-slop ANY scientific text: paper sections, abstracts, proposals, fellowship applications, motivation letter… ([sreenathvemula/finance-research-agent](https://github.com/sreenathvemula/finance-research-agent/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/sci-write))
- **sci-write-revise**: Review the sentence-level writing quality of existing scientific prose (thesis chapters, reports, papers) by editing it in place. Use when asked to review, check, tighten, or clean up writing, strip clutter, fix passive voice or nominalizations, or check terminology and number consistency. Do not use for drafting new text (use scientific-writing), technical review of methods or results, or citation formatting. ([devWhyqueue/master-thesis](https://github.com/devWhyqueue/master-thesis/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/sci-write-revise))
